**Updated:** 9/24/2026, 8:10:59 PM

---

## User

Erstelle in diesem leeren Verzeichnis eine kleine, produktionsnahe PWA namens **PulseBoard**.

Ziel:
Eine minimalistische persönliche Tagesübersicht, in der Nutzer kleine Aktivitäten oder Gewohnheiten abhaken und ihren Tagesfortschritt sehen können.

Technische Anforderungen:

* React
* TypeScript
* Vite
* PWA-fähig
* Keine Backend-API
* Keine externen Webdienste
* Daten lokal im Browser speichern
* Die Anwendung muss offline funktionieren
* Responsive für Desktop und Smartphone

Funktionen:

1. Aktivitäten verwalten

* Aktivität hinzufügen
* Aktivität bearbeiten
* Aktivität löschen
* Jede Aktivität hat:

  * Name
  * optionale Beschreibung
  * Kategorie
  * optionales Tagesziel als Zahl

2. Tagesstatus

* Aktivitäten für den aktuellen Tag abhaken
* Abgehakte Einträge müssen nach einem Reload erhalten bleiben
* Der Status verschiedener Kalendertage darf sich nicht gegenseitig überschreiben

3. Dashboard
   Zeige:

* Anzahl erledigter Aktivitäten
* Anzahl offener Aktivitäten
* Fortschritt in Prozent
* einfache Übersicht nach Kategorie

4. Tagesnavigation

* Heute
* vorheriger Tag
* nächster Tag
* Ein zukünftiger Tag darf angezeigt werden, aber Aktivitäten dort dürfen noch nicht als erledigt markiert werden

5. Persistenz

* Verwende localStorage oder IndexedDB
* Die Datenstruktur soll versioniert sein
* Ungültige oder veraltete gespeicherte Daten dürfen die App nicht zum Absturz bringen

6. PWA

* Manifest
* installierbar
* sinnvoller App-Name und Icons bzw. generierbare Platzhalter
* Service Worker
* App-Shell soll offline funktionieren

7. UX

* sauberes, modernes und ruhiges Design
* Dark Mode berücksichtigen
* Tastaturbedienung nicht vergessen
* sinnvolle Empty States
* sinnvolle Fehlermeldungen
* keine unnötigen Animationen

8. Qualität

* automatisierte Tests schreiben
* insbesondere testen:

  * Erstellen einer Aktivität
  * Bearbeiten
  * Löschen
  * Tagesstatus
  * Datumswechsel
  * Persistenz
  * beschädigte gespeicherte Daten
* TypeScript ohne offensichtliche Typfehler
* Linting
* Production Build

Arbeitsweise:

* Arbeite selbstständig.
* Stelle keine Rückfragen.
* Entscheide selbst über eine sinnvolle Projektstruktur.
* Führe Tests, Lint und Production Build selbst aus.
* Wenn etwas fehlschlägt, analysiere die Ursache und korrigiere sie.
* Verifiziere am Ende den tatsächlichen Zustand der Anwendung.
* Beende die Aufgabe erst, wenn Tests und Build erfolgreich sind.

Wichtig:
Verwende keine MCPs, keine Websuche und keine externen Hilfsmittel außer den lokalen Projekt- und Terminalwerkzeugen.

```ts
export const STORAGE_KEY = 'pulseboard';
export const STORAGE_VERSION = 1;

export function createEmptyData(): StorageData {
  return { version: STORAGE_VERSION, activities: {}, completions: {} };
}

export function readStored(): StorageData | null {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return createEmptyData();
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    const migrated = migrate(parsed);
    if (migrated === null) return null; // corrupt
    return migrated;
  } catch {
    return null; // corruption -> safe
  }
}

function migrate(parsed: unknown): StorageData | null {
  // validate shape
  if (typeof parsed !== 'object' || parsed === null) return null;
  const p = parsed as any;
  // accept version 0 or 1
  let version = typeof p.version === 'number' ? p.version : STORAGE_VERSION;
  // normalize
  ...
  return normalized;
}
```

Careful with DST: adding days by setting time and adding 86400000 ms can shift across DST. Better to parse Y-M-D and use setUTCDate on a UTC date to avoid DST. I'll implement using UTC components to compute keys deterministically.

`dateToKey(d: Date)`: use d.getFullYear, getMonth+1, getDate, zero-pad → local date string.
`keyToDate(key)`: parse into a Date at local midnight: new Date(y, m-1, d).
`addDays(key, n)`: const d = keyToDate(key); d.setDate(d.getDate()+n); return dateToKey(d). Using local setDate avoids DST issues.
`todayKey()`: dateToKey(new Date()).

Let me implement factory with subscribe:

```ts
export function createStore(backend = defaultBackend, time = () => new Date()): Store {
  let state = readState(backend); // { data, selectedDate }
  const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

  function persist() {
    try { backend.set(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state.data)); }
    catch { /* ignore quota/errors */ }
  }

  function emit() { listeners.forEach(l => l()); }

  const store = {
    get state() { return state; },
    subscribe(fn) { ... },
    setSelectedDate(d) { state = {...state, selectedDate: d}; emit(); },
    addActivity(input) { ... persist(); emit(); return activity; },
    editActivity(id, input) { ... },
    deleteActivity(id) { ... },
    toggleComplete(activityId, date) { ... },
    reload() { state = readState(backend); emit(); },
  };
  return store;
}
```

```ts
function isValidActivity(x: unknown): x is Activity {
  if (typeof x !== 'object' || x === null) return false;
  const a = x as any;
  return typeof a.id === 'string' && typeof a.name === 'string'
    && typeof a.description === 'string'
    && typeof a.category === 'string'
    && typeof a.dailyTarget === 'number' && Number.isFinite(a.dailyTarget)
    && typeof a.createdAt === 'string';
}

function parseCompletions(x): Completions { ... }
```

migrate(parsed):
```ts
if (typeof parsed !== 'object' || parsed === null) return null;
const p = parsed as any;
if (Array.isArray(p)) return null;
const version = typeof p.version === 'number' ? p.version : STORAGE_VERSION;
if (version === STORAGE_VERSION) {
  const activities = normalizeActivities(p.activities);
  const completions = normalizeCompletions(p.completions);
  if (activities === null || completions === null) return null;
  return { version: STORAGE_VERSION, activities, completions };
}
if (version === 0) {
  // array-based legacy
  const activities = legacyActivitiesToMap(p.activities); // may be null
  const completions = legacyCompletionsToMap(p.completions);
  if (activities === null || completions === null) return null;
  return { version: STORAGE_VERSION, activities, completions };
}
// unknown version -> treat as corrupt? or safe empty? 
return null;
```

- parseStored(raw):
  - if !raw → createEmptyData()

normalizeActivities(x):
  - if x == null → {}
  - entries = Array.isArray(x) ? Object.values(x) : (typeof x==='object' ? Object.values(x) : [])
  - result = {}

  - return result   // never null

normalizeCompletions(x):

  - result = {}

      - counts = {}

  - return result

Now store actions operate on state.data.

addActivity(input):
  - validate input (name non-empty trimmed, category non-empty, dailyTarget >=0 number)
  - if invalid → return null (caller shows error)
  - id = crypto.randomUUID() (or fallback)
  - activity = { id, name, description, category, dailyTarget, createdAt: now().toISOString() }
  - state.data.activities[id] = activity
  - persist, emit
  - return activity

editActivity(id, input):
  - existing = activities[id]; if !existing return false
  - validate; if invalid return false
  - merge: name, description, category, dailyTarget updated
  - persist, emit; return true

deleteActivity(id):
  - delete activities[id]
  - remove from all completions (any date referencing id) — to avoid orphan counts; optional but clean
  - persist, emit

toggleComplete(activityId, date):
  - activity = activities[activityId]; if !activity return
  - if isFuture(date, time()) return  // can't complete future
  - target = max(0, round(dailyTarget))
  - current = completions[date]?.[activityId] ?? 0
  - let next
  - if target > 0: next = current < target ? current+1 : current-1
  - else: next = current > 0 ? 0 : 1
  - if next <= 0: remove completions[date][activityId] (cleanup)
  - else: completions[date][activityId] = next
  - persist, emit

Note: toggling when current==target and target>0 decrements to target-1 (never 0 unless target 1). That's fine — stepping down. Good.

```ts
function useAppStore() {
  const [store] = useState(() => createStore());
  const [state, setState] = useState(() => store.getState());
  useEffect(() => store.subscribe(() => setState(store.getState())), [store]);

}

Let me just return the store and in App call store.getState() via a subscribed snapshot. Simpler: hook returns state + actions bound:

```ts
export function useAppStore() {
  const store = useMemo(() => createStore(), []);
  const [state, setState] = useState(() => store.getState());
  useEffect(() => store.subscribe(setState), [store]);
  return {
    ...state,
    addActivity: (i) => store.addActivity(i),
    editActivity: (i, x) => store.editActivity(i, x),
    deleteActivity: (id) => store.deleteActivity(id),
    toggleComplete: (a, d) => store.toggleComplete(a, d),
    setSelectedDate: (d) => store.setSelectedDate(d),
    reload: () => store.reload(),
  };
}
```

**Input:**
```json
{
  "content": "/**\n * Hilfsfunktionen für Datumsberechnungen.\n * Intern werden Datumsketten im Format YYYY-MM-DD (lokal) verwendet, damit\n * verschiedene Kalendertage sauber getrennt bleiben und es durch\n * Zeitzonen-/Sommerzeit-Umbuchungen keine Randfälle gibt.\n */\n\n/** Liefert den aktuellen Tag als YYYY-MM-DD-Kette (lokal). */\nexport function todayKey(date: Date = new Date()): string {\n  return dateToKey(date)\n}\n\n/** Wandelt ein lokales Date-Objekt in eine YYYY-MM-DD-Kette um. */\nexport function dateToKey(date: Date): string {\n  const y = date.getFullYear()\n  const m = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')\n  const d = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')\n  return `${y}-${m}-${d}`\n}\n\n/** Wandelt eine YYYY-MM-DD-Kette in ein lokales Date (Mitternacht) um. */\nexport function keyToDate(key: string): Date {\n  const [y, m, d] = key.split('-').map(Number)\n  // Monatsindex in JS ist 0-basiert.\n  return new Date(y, m - 1, d)\n}\n\n/** Addiert (oder subtrahiert) ganze Tage zu einer Kette. vermeidet DST-Sprünge. */\nexport function addDays(key: string, days: number): string {\n  const date = keyToDate(key)\n  date.setDate(date.getDate() + days)\n  return dateToKey(date)\n}\n\n/** Ist der Tag strictly nach dem heutigen Tag? */\nexport function isFuture(key: string, today: string = todayKey()): boolean {\n  return key > today\n}\n\n/** Ist der Tag der heutige? */\nexport function isToday(key: string, today: string = todayKey()): boolean {\n  return key === today\n}\n\n/**\n * Menschlich lesbares Label für einen Tag relativ zu \"heute\".\n * Nutzt Intl.DateTimeFormat für eine saubere, lokale Formatierung.\n */\nexport function formatDayLabel(key: string, today: string = todayKey()): string {\n  const date = keyToDate(key)\n  const opts: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions = {\n    weekday: 'long',\n    day: 'numeric',\n    month: 'long',\n  }\n\n  if (isToday(key, today)) {\n    return 'Heute'\n  }\n\n  const yesterday = addDays(today, -1)\n  const tomorrow = addDays(today, 1)\n  if (key === yesterday) return 'Gestern'\n  if (key === tomorrow) return 'Morgen'\n\n  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('de-DE', opts).format(date)\n}\n\n/** Saubere, kurze Formatierung z. B. für die Navigationsleiste. */\nexport function formatShort(key: string): string {\n  const date = keyToDate(key)\n  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('de-DE', {\n    day: '2-digit',\n    month: '2-digit',\n    year: 'numeric',\n  }).format(date)\n}\n",
  "path": "src/lib/date.ts"
}

Now the storage module with versioned parsing, migration, and corruption safety:



**Tool: write**

**Input:**
```json
{
  "content": "/**\n * Persistenzschicht für PulseBoard.\n *\n * Verantwortlichkeiten:\n * - Lesen/Schreiben einer versionierten Datenstruktur in einem Storage-Backend\n *   (Standard: localStorage).\n * - Migration von älteren Schema-Versionen (aktuell v0 -> v1).\n * - Robustheit: ungültige, veraltete oder beschädigte Daten dürfen nie zum\n *   Absturz führen. Stattdessen wird sicher ein leerer Ausgangszustand\n *   verwendet.\n */\n\nimport type { Activity, Completions, NewActivityInput, StorageData } from '../types'\nimport { STORAGE_KEY, STORAGE_VERSION } from '../types'\n\n/** Minimaler Storage-Contract, um Tests mit einem In-Memory-Backend zu ermöglichen. */\nexport interface StorageBackend {\n  get(key: string): string | null\n  set(key: string, value: string): void\n  remove(key: string): void\n}\n\nexport const defaultBackend: StorageBackend = {\n  get: (key) => localStorage.getItem(key),\n  set: (key, value) => localStorage.setItem(key, value),\n  remove: (key) => localStorage.removeItem(key),\n}\n\n/** Erstellt einen sauberen, leeren Ausgangszustand der aktuellen Version. */\nexport function createEmptyData(): StorageData {\n  return { version: STORAGE_VERSION, activities: {}, completions: {} }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Validierung & Migration\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction isValidActivity(value: unknown): value is Activity {\n  if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) {\n    return false\n  }\n  const a = value as Record<string, unknown>\n  return (\n    typeof a.id === 'string' &&\n    typeof a.name === 'string' &&\n    typeof a.description === 'string' &&\n    typeof a.category === 'string' &&\n    typeof a.dailyTarget === 'number' &&\n    Number.isFinite(a.dailyTarget) &&\n    typeof a.createdAt === 'string'\n  )\n}\n\nfunction normalizeActivities(value: unknown): Record<string, Activity> {\n  const result: Record<string, Activity> = {}\n  if (value == null) {\n    return result\n  }\n  const entries = Array.isArray(value) ? value : Object.values(value as object)\n  for (const entry of entries) {\n    if (isValidActivity(entry)) {\n      // Sicherheit: Tagesziel nie negativ.\n      entry.dailyTarget = Math.max(0, entry.dailyTarget)\n      result[entry.id] = entry\n    }\n  }\n  return result\n}\n\nfunction normalizeCompletions(value: unknown): Completions {\n  const result: Completions = {}\n  if (value == null || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) {\n    return result\n  }\n  for (const [date, perDay] of Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)) {\n    if (typeof perDay !== 'object' || perDay == null || Array.isArray(perDay)) {\n      continue\n    }\n    const counts: Record<string, number> = {}\n    for (const [activityId, count] of Object.entries(perDay as Record<string, unknown>)) {\n      if (typeof count === 'number' && Number.isFinite(count) && count > 0) {\n        counts[activityId] = Math.floor(count)\n      }\n    }\n    if (Object.keys(counts).length > 0) {\n      result[date] = counts\n    }\n  }\n  return result\n}\n\n/**\n * Konvertiert eine v0-Datenstruktur (Array-basiert) in das v1-Objekt-Format.\n * Gibt `null` zurück, wenn die Grundstruktur nicht verwendbar ist.\n */\nfunction migrateV0(parsed: Record<string, unknown>): StorageData | null {\n  const activities = normalizeActivities(parsed.activities)\n  const completions = normalizeCompletions(parsed.completions)\n  return { version: STORAGE_VERSION, activities, completions }\n}\n\n/**\n * Validiert und migriert einen geparsten Wert.\n * Gibt `null` zurück, wenn die Struktur grundsätzlich unhambarrlich ist\n * (z. B. Zahl, String, Array auf Top-Ebene, unbekannte Version).\n */\nexport function parseStored(raw: string | null): StorageData | null {\n  if (!raw) {\n    return createEmptyData()\n  }\n\n  let parsed: unknown\n  try {\n    parsed = JSON.parse(raw)\n  } catch {\n    // Beschädigtes JSON -> sicher leer, kein Absturz.\n    return null\n  }\n\n  if (typeof parsed !== 'object' || parsed === null || Array.isArray(parsed)) {\n    return null\n  }\n\n  const record = parsed as Record<string, unknown>\n  const version = typeof record.version === 'number' ? record.version : STORAGE_VERSION\n\n  if (version === STORAGE_VERSION) {\n    const activities = normalizeActivities(record.activities)\n    const completions = normalizeCompletions(record.completions)\n    return { version: STORAGE_VERSION, activities, completions }\n  }\n\n  if (version === 0) {\n    return migrateV0(record)\n  }\n\n  // Unbekannte/zukünftige Version -> zurücksetzen, aber nicht abstürzen.\n  return null\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Lese-/Schreiboperationen\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/** Liest und normalisiert die gespeicherten Daten aus dem Backend. */\nexport function readStored(backend: StorageBackend = defaultBackend): StorageData {\n  const parsed = parseStored(backend.getItem(STORAGE_KEY))\n  return parsed ?? createEmptyData()\n}\n\n/** Schreibt die Daten serialisiert ins Backend und fängt Fehler ab. */\nexport function writeStored(data: StorageData, backend: StorageBackend = defaultBackend): void {\n  try {\n    backend.set(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data))\n  } catch {\n    // Z. B. Speicherlimit oder gesperrter Storage: nicht kritisch für die App.\n  }\n}\n\nexport function clearStored(backend: StorageBackend = defaultBackend): void {\n  try {\n    backend.remove(STORAGE_KEY)\n  } catch {\n    // ignore\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Validierung von Aktivitäts-Eingaben\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport interface ValidatedActivity {\n  ok: true\n  activity: Activity\n}\n\nexport interface InvalidActivity {\n  ok: false\n  error: string\n}\n\nexport type ValidationResult = ValidatedActivity | InvalidActivity\n\n/**\n * Prüft und normalisiert eine Aktivitäts-Eingabe.\n * Gibt eine Fehlermeldung zurück, wenn die Eingabe ungült ist.\n */\nexport function validateActivity(\n  input: NewActivityInput,\n  overrides: Partial<Activity>,\n): ValidationResult {\n  const name = (input.name ?? '').trim()\n  const category = (input.category ?? '').trim()\n  const description = (input.description ?? '').trim()\n\n  let dailyTarget = 0\n  if (input.dailyTarget !== undefined && input.dailyTarget !== null) {\n    const value = Number(input.dailyTarget)\n    if (Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0) {\n      dailyTarget = Math.floor(value)\n    } else {\n      return { ok: false, error: 'Tagesziel muss eine Zahl größer oder gleich 0 sein.' }\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (name.length === 0) {\n    return { ok: false, error: 'Bitte gib einen Name ein.' }\n  }\n  if (category.length === 0) {\n    return { ok: false, error: 'Bitte wähle eine Kategorie aus.' }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    ok: true,\n    activity: {\n      id: overrides.id ?? crypto.randomUUID(),\n      name,\n      description,\n      category,\n      dailyTarget,\n      createdAt: overrides.createdAt ?? new Date().toISOString(),\n    },\n  }\n}\n",
  "path": "src/lib/storage.ts"
}
```

```json
{
  "content": "/**\n * Reiner Speicher/Logik-Fabrik für PulseBoard (ohne React).\n *\n * Die Fabrik hält den Zustand im Speicher, schreibt nach jeder Änderung persist\n * ins Backend und benachrichtigt Subscriber. Dadurch lässt sich die gesamte\n * Geschäftslogik (Erstellen/Bearbeiten/Löschen, Tagesstatus, Datumswechsel,\n * Persistenz, umgehen mit beschädigten Daten) ohne Framework testen.\n */\n\nimport { addDays, isFuture, todayKey } from '../lib/date'\nimport {\n  clearStored,\n  readStored,\n  StorageBackend,\n  defaultBackend,\n  validateActivity,\n  writeStored,\n} from '../lib/storage'\nimport type { Activity, ActivityInput, Completions, NewActivityInput, StorageData } from '../types'\nimport { STORAGE_KEY } from '../types'\n\nexport interface AppState {\n  data: StorageData\n  selectedDate: string\n}\n\nexport interface Store {\n  getState(): AppState\n  subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void\n  setSelectedDate(date: string): void\n  addActivity(input: NewActivityInput): Activity | null\n  editActivity(id: string, input: ActivityInput): boolean\n  deleteActivity(id: string): void\n  toggleComplete(activityId: string, date: string): void\n  reload(): void\n  reset(): void\n}\n\nexport interface StoreFactoryOptions {\n  backend?: StorageBackend\n  now?: () => Date\n}\n\ninterface Internal {\n  state: AppState\n  backend: StorageBackend\n  now: () => Date\n  listeners: Set<() => void>\n}\n\nfunction computeNow(internal: Internal): string {\n  return todayKey(internal.now())\n}\n\nfunction persist(internal: Internal): void {\n  writeStored(internal.state.data, internal.backend)\n}\n\nfunction emit(internal: Internal): void {\n  for (const listener of internal.listeners) {\n    listener()\n  }\n}\n\nexport function createStore(options: StoreFactoryOptions = {}): Store {\n  const backend = options.backend ?? defaultBackend\n  const now = options.now ?? (() => new Date())\n  const internal: Internal = {\n    state: { data: readStored(backend), selectedDate: computeNow(internal) },\n    backend,\n    now,\n    listeners: new Set(),\n  }\n\n  function setState(next: Partial<AppState>): void {\n    internal.state = { ...internal.state, ...next }\n    persist(internal)\n    emit(internal)\n  }\n\n  return {\n    getState() {\n      return internal.state\n    },\n\n    subscribe(listener) {\n      internal.listeners.add(listener)\n      return () => {\n        internal.listeners.delete(listener)\n      }\n    },\n\n    setSelectedDate(date) {\n      setState({ selectedDate: date })\n    },\n\n    addActivity(input) {\n      const result = validateActivity(input, {})\n      if (!result.ok) {\n        return null\n      }\n      const activity = result.activity\n      internal.state.data.activities[activity.id] = activity\n      persist(internal)\n      emit(internal)\n      return activity\n    },\n\n    editActivity(id, input) {\n      const existing = internal.state.data.activities[id]\n      if (!existing) {\n        return false\n      }\n      const result = validateActivity(input, { id, createdAt: existing.createdAt })\n      if (!result.ok) {\n        return false\n      }\n      internal.state.data.activities[id] = result.activity\n      persist(internal)\n      emit(internal)\n      return true\n    },\n\n    deleteActivity(id) {\n      if (!internal.state.data.activities[id]) {\n        return\n      }\n      delete internal.state.data.activities[id]\n      // Verwaigte Zählstände entfernen.\n      for (const dateKeys of Object.values(internal.state.data.completions)) {\n        delete dateKeys[id]\n      }\n      persist(internal)\n      emit(internal)\n    },\n\n    toggleComplete(activityId, date) {\n      const activity = internal.state.data.activities[activityId]\n      if (!activity) {\n        return\n      }\n      // Zukünftige Tage dürfen nicht als erledigt markiert werden.\n      if (isFuture(date, computeNow(internal))) {\n        return\n      }\n      const target = Math.max(0, activity.dailyTarget)\n      const current = internal.state.data.completions[date]?.[activityId] ?? 0\n\n      let next: number\n      if (target > 0) {\n        next = current < target ? current + 1 : current - 1\n      } else {\n        next = current > 0 ? 0 : 1\n      }\n\n      const dayCompletions = internal.state.data.completions[date] ?? {}\n      if (next <= 0) {\n        delete dayCompletions[activityId]\n        if (Object.keys(dayCompletions).length === 0) {\n          delete internal.state.data.completions[date]\n        }\n      } else {\n        dayCompletions[activityId] = next\n        internal.state.data.completions[date] = dayCompletions\n      }\n      persist(internal)\n      emit(internal)\n    },\n\n    reload() {\n      internal.state = { data: readStored(internal.backend), selectedDate: computeNow(internal) }\n      persist(internal)\n      emit(internal)\n    },\n\n    reset() {\n      clearStored(internal.backend)\n      internal.state = { data: readStored(internal.backend), selectedDate: computeNow(internal) }\n      emit(internal)\n    },\n  }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Hilfsfunktionen für die Ansicht\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/** Anzahl erledigter Einträge einer Aktivität an einem Tag. */\nexport function getCompletedCount(\n  data: StorageData,\n  activityId: string,\n  date: string,\n): number {\n  return data.completions[date]?.[activityId] ?? 0\n}\n\n/** Ist eine Aktivität an einem Tag als (voll) erledigt? */\nexport function isCompleted(data: StorageData, activity: Activity, date: string): boolean {\n  const count = getCompletedCount(data, activity.id, date)\n  if (activity.dailyTarget > 0) {\n    return count >= activity.dailyTarget\n  }\n  return count > 0\n}\n\nexport interface CategoryStat {\n  name: string\n  total: number\n  completed: number\n}\n\nexport interface DayStats {\n  total: number\n  completed: number\n  open: number\n  percent: number\n  byCategory: CategoryStat[]\n}\n\n/** Berechnet die Dashboard-Statistik für einen Tag. */\nexport function computeStats(data: StorageData, date: string): DayStats {\n  const activities = Object.values(data.activities)\n  const total = activities.length\n  let completed = 0\n  const byCategoryMap = new Map<string, { total: number; completed: number }>()\n\n  for (const activity of activities) {\n    const done = isCompleted(data, activity, date)\n    if (done) {\n      completed += 1\n    }\n    const cat = activity.category || 'Ohne Kategorie'\n    const entry = byCategoryMap.get(cat) ?? { total: 0, completed: 0 }\n    entry.total += 1\n    if (done) {\n      entry.completed += 1\n    }\n    byCategoryMap.set(cat, entry)\n  }\n\n  const byCategory = [...byCategoryMap.entries()]\n    .map(([name, values]) => ({ name, ...values }))\n    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))\n\n  const open = total - completed\n  const percent = total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((completed / total) * 100)\n\n  return { total, completed, open, percent, byCategory }\n}\n\n/** Liefert sortierte, eindeutige Kategorien aus den aktuellen Aktivitäten. */\nexport function listCategories(data: StorageData): string[] {\n  const categories = new Set<string>()\n  for (const activity of Object.values(data.activities)) {\n    if (activity.category) {\n      categories.add(activity.category)\n    }\n  }\n  return [...categories].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b))\n}\n\nexport { addDays, todayKey }\n",
  "path": "src/store/createStore.ts"
}
```

Haltet den Zustand synchron und\n * schreibt Änderungen persist in den Storage.\n */\nexport function useAppStore(): AppState & AppActions {\n  const store = useMemo<Store>(() => createStore(), [])\n\n  const [state, setState] = useState<AppState>(() => store.getState())\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    return store.subscribe(() => setState(store.getState()))\n  }, [store])\n\n  const actions = useMemo<AppActions>(\n    () => ({\n      setSelectedDate: (date) => store.setSelectedDate(date),\n      addActivity: (input) => store.addActivity(input),\n      editActivity: (id, input) => store.editActivity(id, input),\n      deleteActivity: (id) => store.deleteActivity(id),\n      toggleComplete: (activityId, date) => store.toggleComplete(activityId, date),\n      reload: () => store.reload(),\n      reset: () => store.reset(),\n    }),\n    [store],\n  )\n\n  return { ...state, ...actions }\n}\n",
  "path": "src/hooks/useAppStore.ts"
}

{
  "content": "import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'\n\nexport type ThemeChoice = 'system' | 'light' | 'dark'\n\nconst THEME_KEY = 'pulseboard-theme'\n\nfunction getInitialChoice(): ThemeChoice {\n  try {\n    const stored = localStorage.getItem(THEME_KEY)\n    if (stored === 'light' || stored === 'dark' || stored === 'system') {\n      return stored\n    }\n  } catch {\n    // ignore\n  }\n  return 'system'\n}\n\nfunction resolveTheme(choice: ThemeChoice): 'light' | 'dark' {\n  if (choice === 'light') return 'light'\n  if (choice === 'dark') return 'dark'\n  const prefersDark =\n    typeof window !== 'undefined' &&\n    window.matchMedia &&\n    window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches\n  return prefersDark ? 'dark' : 'light'\n}\n\nfunction applyTheme(theme: 'light' | 'dark'): void {\n  const root = document.documentElement\n  root.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)\n  const meta = document.querySelector<metaElement>('meta[name=\"theme-color\"]')\n  if (meta) {\n    meta.content = theme === 'dark' ? '#0f172a' : '#f8fafc'\n  }\n}\n\ninterface metaElement extends HTMLMetaElement {}\n\n/**\n * Verwählt und synchronisiert den Dark/Light-Modus. Standard ist das System-\n * Präferenz, die Wahl wird lokal gespeichert.\n */\nexport function useTheme(): { choice: ThemeChoice; setChoice: (c: ThemeChoice) => void } {\n  const [choice, setChoice] = useState<ThemeChoice>(getInitialChoice)\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const theme = resolveTheme(choice)\n    applyTheme(theme)\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(THEME_KEY, choice)\n    } catch {\n      // ignore\n    }\n  }, [choice])\n\n  return { choice, setChoice }\n}\n",
  "path": "src/hooks/useTheme.ts"
}

{
  "content": "import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'\n\nexport type NotificationType = 'info' | 'success' | 'error'\n\nexport interface Notification {\n  id: string\n  message: string\n  type: NotificationType\n}\n\ninterface NotificationsContextValue {\n  notifications: Notification[]\n  notify: (message: string, type?: NotificationType) => void\n  dismiss: (id: string) => void\n}\n\nconst NotificationsContext = createContext<NotificationsContextValue | null>(null)\n\nlet counter = 0\n\nconst DEFAULT_TTL = 3500\n\nexport function NotificationsProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<Notification[]>([])\n  const timers = useRef<Record<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>>({})\n\n  const dismiss = useCallback((id: string) => {\n    setNotifications((list) => list.filter((n) => n.id !== id))\n    const timer = timers.current[id]\n    if (timer) {\n      clearTimeout(timer)\n      delete timers.current[id]\n    }\n  }, [])\n\n  const notify = useCallback((message: string, type: NotificationType = 'info') => {\n    const id = `n-${Date.now()}-${counter++}`\n    setNotifications((list) => [...list, { id, message, type }])\n    timers.current[id] = setTimeout(() => dismiss(id), DEFAULT_TTL)\n  }, [dismiss])\n\n  const value = useMemo<NotificationsContextValue>(() => ({ notifications, notify, dismiss }), [\n    notifications,\n    notify,\n    dismiss,\n  ])\n\n  return (\n    <NotificationsContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</NotificationsContext.Provider>\n  )\n}\n\nexport function useNotifications(): NotificationsContextValue {\n  const ctx = useContext(NotificationsContext)\n  if (!ctx) {\n    throw new Error('useNotifications muss innerhalb eines NotificationsProvider verwendet werden')\n  }\n  return ctx\n}\n",
  "path": "src/hooks/useNotifications.ts"
}

  "content": "import { useTheme } from '../hooks/useTheme'\nimport { MoonIcon, SunIcon } from './icons'\n\n/** Umschalter zwischen Light-/Dark-Modus (klickbar, keyboard-freundlich). */\nexport function ThemeToggle() {\n  const { choice, setChoice } = useTheme()\n  const isDark = choice === 'dark' || (choice === 'system' && darkModeMatches())\n\n  return (\n    <button\n      type=\"button\"\n      className=\"icon-btn\"\n      onClick={() => setChoice(isDark ? 'light' : 'dark')}\n      aria-label={isDark ? 'Hellen Modus wählen' : 'Dunkelen Modus wählen'}\n      aria-pressed={isDark}\n      title={choice === 'system' ? 'System' : choice}\n    >\n      {isDark ? <MoonIcon /> : <SunIcon />}\n    </button>\n  )\n}\n\nfunction darkModeMatches(): boolean {\n  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !window.matchMedia) return false\n  return window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches\n}\n",
  "path": "src/components/ThemeToggle.tsx"
}
```

{
  "content": "import type { DayStats } from '../store/createStore'\nimport { SparkleIcon } from './icons'\n\ninterface DashboardProps {\n  stats: DayStats\n}\n\n/** Fortschrittsbalken. */\nfunction ProgressBar({ percent }: { percent: number }) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"progress\" role=\"progressbar\" aria-valuenow={percent} aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-label=\"Tagesfortschritt\">\n      <div className=\"progress__track\">\n        <div className=\"progress__fill\" style={{ width: `${percent}%` }} />\n      </div>\n      <span className=\"progress__value\">{percent}%</span>\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n\n/** Übersicht nach Kategorie. */\nfunction CategoryOverview({ byCategory }: { byCategory: DayStats['byCategory'] }) {\n  if (byCategory.length === 0) {\n    return null\n  }\n  return (\n    <div className=\"categories\">\n      <h2 className=\"categories__title\">Kategorien</h2>\n      <ul className=\"categories__list\">\n        {byCategory.map((c) => {\n          const catPercent = c.total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((c.completed / c.total) * 100)\n          return (\n            <li key={c.name} className=\"category\">\n              <div className=\"category__top\">\n                <span className=\"category__name\">{c.name}</span>\n                <span className=\"category__count\">\n                  {c.completed}/{c.total}\n                </span>\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"progress progress--sm\">\n                <div className=\"progress__track\">\n                  <div className=\"progress__fill\" style={{ width: `${catPercent}%` }} />\n                </div>\n                <span className=\"progress__value progress__value--sm\">{catPercent}%</span>\n              </div>\n            </li>\n          )\n        })}\n      </ul>\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n\n/** Dashboard: erledigt/offen/Prozent + Kategorie-Übersicht. */\nexport function Dashboard({ stats }: DashboardProps) {\n  const { completed, open, percent } = stats\n\n  return (\n    <section className=\"dashboard\" aria-label=\"Tagesübersicht\">\n      <div className=\"dashboard__stats\">\n        <Stat icon={<SparkleIcon />} label=\"Erledigt\" value={completed} highlight />\n        <Stat icon={<SparkleIcon />} label=\"Offen\" value={open} />\n        <Stat icon={<SparkleIcon />} label=\"Gesamt\" value={stats.total} />\n      </div>\n\n      <ProgressBar percent={percent} />\n\n      <CategoryOverview byCategory={stats.byCategory} />\n    </section>\n  )\n}\n\nfunction Stat({\n  icon,\n  label,\n  value,\n  highlight,\n}: {\n  icon: React.ReactNode\n  label: string\n  value: number\n  highlight?: boolean\n}) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"stat\" aria-label={label}>\n      <div className=\"stat__icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">\n        {icon}\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"stat__value\" data-highlight={Boolean(highlight)}>\n        {value}\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"stat__label\">{label}</div>\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n",

}

{
  "content": "import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'\nimport { useNotifications } from '../hooks/useNotifications'\nimport { validateActivity } from '../lib/storage'\nimport type { Activity, ActivityInput } from '../types'\nimport { EditIcon, PlusIcon, TrashIcon } from './icons'\n\nexport type FormMode = 'create' | 'edit'\n\ninterface ActivityFormProps {\n  mode: FormMode\n  initial?: Activity\n  categories: string[]\n  onSubmit: (input: ActivityInput) => void\n  onCancel: () => void\n  onDelete?: () => void\n}\n\ninterface FieldState {\n  name: string\n  description: string\n  category: string\n  dailyTarget: string // als Text, um leere Eingänge zu erlauben\n}\n\nconst EMPTY: FieldState = { name: '', description: '', category: '', dailyTarget: '' }\n\n/** Formular zum Erstellen und Bearbeiten von Aktivitäten. */\nexport function ActivityForm({\n  mode,\n  initial,\n  categories,\n  onSubmit,\n  onCancel,\n  onDelete,\n}: ActivityFormProps) {\n  const { notify } = useNotifications()\n  const [fields, setFields] = useState<FieldState>(EMPTY)\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string>('')\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (mode === 'edit' && initial) {\n      setFields({\n        name: initial.name,\n        description: initial.description,\n        category: initial.category,\n        dailyTarget: initial.dailyTarget > 0 ? String(initial.dailyTarget) : '',\n      })\n      setError('')\n    } else {\n      setFields(EMPTY)\n      setError('')\n    }\n  }, [mode, initial])\n\n  function set<K extends keyof FieldState>(key: K, value: FieldState[K]) {\n    setFields((f) => ({ ...f, [key]: value }))\n    if (error) setError('')\n  }\n\n  function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {\n    event.preventDefault()\n    const input: ActivityInput = {\n      name: fields.name,\n      description: fields.description,\n      category: fields.category,\n      dailyTarget: fields.dailyTarget === '' ? undefined : Number(fields.dailyTarget),\n    }\n    const result = validateActivity(input, mode === 'edit' ? { id: initial?.id } : {})\n    if (!result.ok) {\n      setError(result.error)\n      notify(result.error, 'error')\n      return\n    }\n    onSubmit(input)\n  }\n\n  const isEdit = mode === 'edit'\n\n  return (\n    <form className=\"activity-form\" onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>\n      <div className=\"activity-form__row\">\n        <label className=\"field\">\n          <span className=\"field__label\">Name</span>\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            value={fields.name}\n            autoComplete=\"off\"\n            onChange={(e) => set('name', e.target.value)}\n            aria-required=\"true\"\n            aria-invalid={error ? 'true' : undefined}\n            aria-describedby={error ? 'activity-form-error' : undefined}\n            placeholder=\"z. B. Sport, Lesen, Wasser trinken\"\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <label className=\"field\">\n          <span className=\"field__label\">Kategorie</span>\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            list=\"categories\"\n            value={fields.category}\n            onChange={(e) => set('category', e.target.value)}\n            aria-required=\"true\"\n            aria-invalid={error ? 'true' : undefined}\n            placeholder=\"z. B. Gesundheit\"\n          />\n        </label>\n      </div>\n\n      <label className=\"field\">\n        <span className=\"field__label\">Beschreibung <span className=\"field__optional\">(optional)</span></span>\n        <input\n          type=\"text\"\n          value={fields.description}\n          autoComplete=\"off\"\n          onChange={(e) => set('description', e.target.value)}\n          placeholder=\"Was oder warum?\"\n        />\n      </label>\n\n      <div className=\"activity-form__row\">\n        <label className=\"field\">\n          <span className=\"field__label\">Tagesziel <span className=\"field__optional\">(optional)</span></span>\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            inputMode=\"numeric\"\n            min=\"0\"\n            step=\"1\"\n            value={fields.dailyTarget}\n            onChange={(e) => set('dailyTarget', e.target.value)}\n            placeholder=\"0 = kein Ziel\"\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <div className=\"activity-form__actions\">\n          <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn btn--ghost\" onClick={onCancel}>\n            {isEdit ? 'Abbrechen' : 'Abbrechen'}\n          </button>\n          <button type=\"submit\" className=\"btn btn--primary\">\n            {isEdit ? <EditIcon /> : <PlusIcon />}\n            {isEdit ? 'Speichern' : 'Hinzufügen'}\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {isEdit && onDelete && (\n        <button type=\"button\" className=\"btn btn--danger\" onClick={onDelete}>\n          <TrashIcon />\n          Löschen\n        </button>\n      )}\n\n      {error && (\n        <p className=\"activity-form__error\" id=\"activity-form-error\" role=\"alert\">\n          {error}\n        </p>\n      )}\n\n      <datalist id=\"categories\">\n        {categories.map((c) => (\n          <option key={c} value={c} />\n        ))}\n      </datalist>\n    </form>\n  )\n}\n",
  "path": "src/components/ActivityForm.tsx"
}
```

'Zukünftiger Tag'\n    : target\n      ? `${completedCount} / ${target}`\n      : completedCount > 0\n        ? 'Erledigt'\n        : 'Offen'\n\n  return (\n    <li className=\"activity\" data-completed={isCompleted || undefined}>\n      <button\n        type=\"button\"\n        className={`activity__check${isCompleted ? ' is-done' : ''}`}\n        onClick={onToggle}\n        disabled={isFuture}\n        aria-pressed={isCompleted}\n        aria-label={`\"${activity.name}\" für ${statusText} umsetzen`}\n        title={statusText}\n      >\n        {isCompleted && <CheckIcon />}\n      </button>\n\n      <div className=\"activity__main\" onClick={onEdit} style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}>\n        <div className=\"activity__name\" aria-hidden=\"true\">\n          {activity.name}\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"activity__meta\">\n          <span className=\"badge\">{activity.category}</span>\n          {activity.description && <span className=\"activity__desc\">{activity.description}</span>}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"activity__counter\" aria-hidden=\"true\">\n        {target ? (\n          <span className=\"activity__target\">\n            {completedCount}/{target}\n          </span>\n        ) : null}\n      </div>\n\n      <div className=\"activity__actions\">\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          className=\"icon-btn\"\n          onClick={onEdit}\n          aria-label={`\"${activity.name}\" bearbeiten`}\n          title=\"Bearbeiten\"\n        >\n          <EditIcon />\n        </button>\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          className=\"icon-btn icon-btn--danger\"\n          onClick={onDelete}\n          aria-label={`\"${activity.name}\" löschen`}\n          title=\"Löschen\"\n        >\n          <span aria-hidden=\"true\">✕</span>\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </li>\n  )\n}\n",

}

{
  "content": "import { getCompletedCount, isCompleted } from '../store/createStore'\nimport type { Activity, StorageData } from '../types'\nimport { ActivityItem } from './ActivityItem'\n\ninterface ActivityListProps {\n  activities: Activity[]\n  date: string\n  data: StorageData\n  onToggle: (activityId: string) => void\n  onEdit: (activityId: string) => void\n  onDelete: (activityId: string) => void\n}\n\n/** Liste aller Aktivitäten für den ausgewählten Tag. */\nexport function ActivityList({\n  activities,\n  date,\n  data,\n  onToggle,\n  onEdit,\n  onDelete,\n}: ActivityListProps) {\n  if (activities.length === 0) {\n    return null\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <ul className=\"activity-list\">\n      {activities.map((activity) => (\n        <ActivityItem\n          key={activity.id}\n          activity={activity}\n          completedCount={getCompletedCount(data, activity.id, date)}\n          isCompleted={isCompleted(data, activity, date)}\n          isFuture={date > todayKey()}\n          onToggle={() => onToggle(activity.id)}\n          onEdit={() => onEdit(activity.id)}\n          onDelete={() => onDelete(activity.id)}\n        />\n      ))}\n    </ul>\n  )\n}\n\n// Local import to keep the file self-contained for the future-day check.\nimport { todayKey } from '../lib/date'\n",

}

import { useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { ActivityForm } from './components/ActivityForm'

  const [showAdd, setShowAdd] = useState(false)
  const addRef = useRef<HTMLDetailsElement>(null)

  const activities = Object.values(data.activities).sort((a,b) => a.createdAt.localeCompare(b.createdAt))
  const categories = listCategories(data)
  const stats = computeStats(data, selectedDate)

  function handleAdd(input: ActivityInput) {
    const created = addActivity(input)
    if (created) {
      notify('Aktivität hinzugefügt', 'success')
      setShowAdd(false)
    }
  }
  ...
}

handleDelete(id: string) {\n    deleteActivity(id)\n    notify('Aktivität gelöscht', 'info')\n    setEditId(null)\n  }\n\n  function handleToggle(id: string) {\n    toggleComplete(id, selectedDate)\n : undefined\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app\">\n      <div className=\"app__inner\">\n        <Header />\n        <DayNavigator selectedDate={selectedDate} onSelectDate={setSelectedDate} var(--primary);\n  cursor: pointer;\n  list-style: none;\n  font-size: 15px;\n}\n\n.add-panel__summary::-webkit-details-marker {\n  display: none;\n}\n\n.add-panel__summary::before {\n  content: '+';\n  display: inline-grid;\n  place-items: center;\n  width: 22px;\n  height: 22px;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  background: var(--primary-weak);\n  color: var(--primary);\n  font-size: 16px;\n  line-height: 1;\n}\n\n.add-panel[open] .add-panel__summary::before {\n  content: '–';\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Forms\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n.field {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 6px;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n}\n\n.field__optional {\n  font-weight: 400;\n  opacity: 0.7;\n}\n\n.activity-form {\n  padding: 4px 16px 16px;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 14px;\n}\n\n.activity-form__row {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;\n  gap: 12px;\n}\n\n.activity-form__actions {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 8px;\n  justify-content: flex-end;\n}\n\n.activity-form input {\n  width: 100%;\n}\n\ninput {\n  font-size: 15px;\n  padding: 10px 12px;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\n  background: var(--bg-elevated);\n  color: var(--text);\n  transition: border-color 0.12s ease, box-shadow 0.12s ease;\n}\n\ninput:focus {\n  outline: none;\n  border-color: var(--primary);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px var(--primary-weak);\n}\n\n.activity-form__error {\n  margin: 0;\n  color: var(--danger);\n  font-size: 13px;\n  font-weight: 500;\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Activity list\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n.activity-list {\n  list-style: none;\n  margin: 0;\n  padding: 0;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 10px;\n}\n\n.activity {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 12px;\n  background: var(--surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius);\n  padding: 14px;\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);\n}\n\n.activity__check {\n  display: inline-grid;\n  place-items: center;\n  width: 30px;\n  height: 30px;\n  border-radius: 8px;\n  border: 2px solid var(--border-strong);\n  background: var(--bg-elevated);\n  color: #fff;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  flex-shrink: 0;\n  transition: background-color 0.12s ease, border-color 0.12s ease, color 0.12s ease;\n}\n\n.activity__check:hover {\n  border-color: var(--primary);\n}\n\n.activity__check.is-done {\n  background: var(--success);\n  border-color: var(--success);\n}\n\n.activity__check:disabled {\n  opacity: 0.4;\n  cursor: not-allowed;\n}\n\n.activity__main {\n  flex: 1;\n  min-width: 0;\n}\n\n.activity__name {\n  font-weight: 600;\n  font-size: 15px;\n  overflow: hidden;\n  text-overflow: ellipsis;\n  white-space: nowrap;\n}\n\n.activity[data-completed='true'] .activity__name {\n  text-decoration: line-through;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n}\n\n.activity__meta {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  gap: 8px;\n  align-items: center;\n  margin-top: 3px;\n}\n\n.activity__desc {\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  font-size: 13px;\n  overflow: hidden;\n  text-overflow: ellipsis;\n  white-space: nowrap;\n}\n\n.badge {\n  font-size: 11px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  padding: 3px 8px;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  background: var(--primary-weak);\n  color: var(--primary);\n  white-space: nowrap;\n}\n\n.activity__counter {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n}\n\n.activity__target {\n  font-size: 13px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;\n}\n\n.activity__actions {\n  display: flex;\n  gap: 6px;\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Empty state\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n.empty-state {\n  text-align: center;\n  padding: 48px 24px;\n  background: var(--surface);\n  border: 1px dashed var(--border-strong);\n  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n}\n\n.empty-state__icon {\n  display: inline-grid;\n  place-items: center;\n  width: 64px;\n  height: 64px;\n  margin: 0 auto 16px;\n  border-radius: 18px;\n  background: var(--surface-alt);\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n}\n\n.empty-state__title {\n  color: var(--text);\n  font-size: 18px;\n  margin-bottom: 6px;\n}\n\n.empty-state__desc {\n  font-size: 14px;\n  max-width: 40ch;\n  margin: 0 auto 18px;\n}\n\n.empty-state__action {\n  display: flex;\n  justify-content: center;\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Dialog (edit)\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n.dialog-backdrop {\n  position: fixed;\n  inset: 0;\n  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.5);\n  display: grid;\n  place-items: center;\n  padding: 16px;\n  z-index: 50;\n  animation: fade 0.12s ease;\n}\n\n@keyframes fade {\n  from {\n    opacity: 0;\n  }\n  to {\n    opacity: 1;\n  }\n}\n\n.dialog {\n  width: 100%;\n  max-width: 440px;\n  background: var(--bg-elevated);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);\n  padding: 20px;\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow-md);\n  animation: pop 0.14s ease;\n}\n\n@keyframes pop {\n  from {\n    transform: translateY(8px) scale(0.98);\n    opacity: 0;\n  }\n  to {\n    transform: none;\n    opacity: 1;\n  }\n}\n\n.dialog__title {\n  font-size: 17px;\n  margin-bottom: 14px;\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Toasts\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n.toasts {\n  position: fixed;\n  bottom: 20px;\n  left: 50%;\n  transform: translateX(-50%);\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 8px;\n  z-index: 60;\n  width: calc(100% - 32px);\n  max-width: 380px;\n}\n\n.toast {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 12px;\n  border: 1px solid transparent;\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\n  padding: 12px 14px;\n  font-size: 14px;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow-md);\n  color: var(--text);\n  background: var(--surface);\n  border-color: var(--border);\n}\n\n.toast--success {\n  background: var(--success-weak);\n  border-color: var(--success);\n  color: var(--success);\n}\n\n.toast--error {\n  background: var(--danger-weak);\n  border-color: var(--danger);\n  color: var(--danger);\n}\n\n.toast__close {\n  font-size: 18px;\n  line-height: 1;\n  opacity: 0.7;\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Hint\n *---------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n.hint {\n  margin: 0;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--warning);\n  background: var(--warning);\n  color: #fff;\n  padding: 10px 14px;\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\n}\n\n/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Responsive\n 15px;\n  }\n}\n\n@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {\n  * {\n    animation-duration: 0.001ms !important;\n    transition-duration: 0.001ms !important;\n  }\n}\n",

function memoryBackend() {
  let store: Record<string,string> = {}
  return {
    backend: { get:(k)=>k in store?store[k]:null, set:(k,v)=>{store[k]=v}, remove:(k)=>{delete store[k]} },
    snapshot: () => store,
  }
}
```

  "content": "import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'\nimport { addDays, dateToKey, keyToDate, formatDayLabel, isFuture, isToday, todayKey } from './date'\n\ndescribe('date helpers', () => {\n  it('formattelt lokale Datums in YYYY-MM-DD', () => {\n    expect(dateToKey(new Date(2026, 0, 5))).toBe('2026-01-05')\n    expect(dateToKey(new Date(2026, 11, 31))).toBe('2026-12-31')\n  })\n\n  it('wandelt Ketten zurück in Dates', () => {\n    const d = keyToDate('2026-01-05')\n    expect(d.getFullYear()).toBe(2026)\n    expect(d.getMonth()).toBe(0)\n    expect(d.getDate()).toBe(5)\n  })\n\n  it('addiert und subtrahiert Tage', () => {\n    expect(addDays('2026-01-05', 1)).toBe('2026-01-06')\n    expect(addDays('2026-01-05', -1)).toBe('2026-01-04')\n    expect(addDays('2026-01-01', 30)).toBe('2026-01-31')\n  })\n\n  it('erkennt Zukunft und heute', () => {\n    const today = '2026-09-24'\n    expect(isFuture('2026-09-25', today)).toBe(true)\n    expect(isFuture('2026-09-23', today)).toBe(false)\n    expect(isFuture('2026-09-24', today)).toBe(false)\n    expect(isToday('2026-09-24', today)).toBe(true)\n    expect(isToday('2026-09-25', today)).toBe(false)\n  })\n\n  it('liefert relative Labels', () => {\n    const today = '2026-09-24'\n    expect(formatDayLabel('2026-09-24', today)).toBe('Heute')\n    expect(formatDayLabel('2026-09-23', today)).toBe('Gestern')\n    expect(formatDayLabel('2026-09-25', today)).toBe('Morgen')\n    expect(formatDayLabel('2026-09-28', today)).toMatch(/September/)\n  })\n\n  it('todayKey nutzt das übergebene Datum', () => {\n    expect(todayKey(new Date(2026, 8, 24))).toBe('2026-09-24')\n  })\n})\n",
  "path": "src/lib/date.test.ts"
}

renderHook(() => useAppStore())\n\n    act(() => {\n      result.addActivity({ name: 'Sport', category: 'G', dailyTarget: 1 })\n    })\n    expect(Object.keys(result.getState().data.activities).length).toBe(1)\n\n    const id = Object.keys(result.getState().data.activities)[0]\n    act(() => {\n      result.editActivity(id, { name: 'Fitness', category: 'Gesundheit', dailyTarget: 2 })\n    })\n    expect(result.getState().data.activities[id].name).toBe('Fitness')\n\n    act(() => {\n      result.deleteActivity(id)\n    })\n    expect(result.getState().data.activities[id]).toBeUndefined()\n  })\n\n  it('speichert den Tagesstatus und überlebt einen Reload', () => {\n    const { result, rerender } = renderHook(() => useAppStore())\n    const date = result.getState().selectedDate\n\n    act(() => {\n      result.addActivity({ name: 'Wasser', category: 'G', dailyTarget: 0 })\n    })\n    const id = Object.keys(result.getState().data.activities)[0]\n\n    act(() => {\n      result.toggleComplete(id, date)\n    })\n    expect(getCompletedCount(result.getState().data, id, date)).toBe(1)\n\n    //   "content": "import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'\nimport { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react'\nimport userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event'\nimport { App } from '../App'\nimport { NotificationsProvider } from '../hooks/useNotifications'\nimport { STORAGE_KEY } from '../types'\n\nfunction renderApp() {\n  render(\n    <NotificationsProvider>\n      <App />\n    </NotificationsProvider>,\n  )\n}\n\nasync function addActivity(user: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof userEvent.setup>>, name: string, category: string) {\n  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /neue aktivität/i }))\n  const panel = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /neue aktivität/i }).closest('[class]')\n  // Formular innerhalb des Panels bedienen.\n  const nameInput = 1: /**
2:  * Reiner Speicher/Logik-Fabrik für PulseBoard (ohne React).
3:  *
4:  * Die Fabrik hält den Zustand im Speicher, schreibt nach jeder Änderung persist
5:  * ins Backend und benachrichtigt Subscriber. Dadurch lässt sich die gesamte
6:  * Geschäftslogik (Erstellen/Bearbeiten/Löschen, Tagesstatus, Datumswechsel,
7:  * Persistenz, umgehen mit beschädigten Daten) ohne Framework testen.
8:  */
9: 
10: import { addDays, isFuture, todayKey } from '../lib/date'
11: import {
12:   clearStored,
13:   readStored,
14:   StorageBackend,
15:   defaultBackend,
16:   validateActivity,
17:   writeStored,
18: } from '../lib/storage'
19: import type { Activity, ActivityInput, Completions, NewActivityInput, StorageData } from '../types'
20: import { STORAGE_KEY } from '../types'
21: 
22: export interface AppState {
23:   data: StorageData
24:   selectedDate: string
25: }
26: 
27: export interface Store {
28:   getState(): AppState
29:   subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void
30:   setSelectedDate(date: string): void
31:   addActivity(input: NewActivityInput): Activity | null
32:   editActivity(id: string, input: ActivityInput): boolean
33:   deleteActivity(id: string): void
34:   toggleComplete(activityId: string, date: string): void
35:   reload(): void
36:   reset(): void
37: }
38: 
39: export interface StoreFactoryOptions {
40:   backend?: StorageBackend
41:   now?: () => Date
42: }
43: 
44: interface Internal {
45:   state: AppState
46:   backend: StorageBackend
47:   now: () => Date
48:   listeners: Set<() => void>
49: }
50: 
51: function computeNow(internal: Internal): string {
52:   return todayKey(internal.now())
53: }
54: 
55: function persist(internal: Internal): void {
56:   writeStored(internal.state.data, internal.backend)
57: }
58: 
59: function emit(internal: Internal): void {
60:   for (const listener of internal.listeners) {
61:     listener()
62:   }
63: }
64: 
65: export function createStore(options: StoreFactoryOptions = {}): Store {
66:   const backend = options.backend ?? defaultBackend
67:   const now = options.now ?? (() => new Date())
68:   const internal: Internal = {
69:     state: { data: readStored(backend), selectedDate: computeNow(internal) },
70:     backend,
71:     now,
72:     listeners: new Set(),
73:   }
74: 
75:   function setState(next: Partial<AppState>): void {
76:     internal.state = { ...internal.state, ...next }
77:     persist(internal)
78:     emit(internal)
79:   }
80: 
81:   return {
82:     getState() {
83:       return internal.state
84:     },
85: 
86:     subscribe(listener) {
87:       internal.listeners.add(listener)
88:       return () => {
89:         internal.listeners.delete(listener)
90:       }
91:     },
92: 
93:     setSelectedDate(date) {
94:       setState({ selectedDate: date })
95:     },
96: 
97:     addActivity(input) {
98:       const result = validateActivity(input, {})
99:       if (!result.ok) {
100:         return null
101:       }
102:       const activity = result.activity
103:       internal.state.data.activities[activity.id] = activity
104:       persist(internal)
105:       emit(internal)
106:       return activity
107:     },
108: 
109:     editActivity(id, input) {
110:       const existing = internal.state.data.activities[id]
111:       if (!existing) {
112:         return false
113:       }
114:       const result = validateActivity(input, { id, createdAt: existing.createdAt })
115:       if (!result.ok) {
116:         return false
117:       }
118:       internal.state.data.activities[id] = result.activity
119:       persist(internal)
120:       emit(internal)
121:       return true
122:     },
123: 
124:     deleteActivity(id) {
125:       if (!internal.state.data.activities[id]) {
126:         return
127:       }
128:       delete internal.state.data.activities[id]
129:       // Verwaigte Zählstände entfernen.
130:       for (const dateKeys of Object.values(internal.state.data.completions)) {
131:         delete dateKeys[id]
132:       }
133:       persist(internal)
134:       emit(internal)
135:     },
136: 
137:     toggleComplete(activityId, date) {
138:       const activity = internal.state.data.activities[activityId]
139:       if (!activity) {
140:         return
141:       }
142:       // Zukünftige Tage dürfen nicht als erledigt markiert werden.
143:       if (isFuture(date, computeNow(internal))) {
144:         return
145:       }
146:       const target = Math.max(0, activity.dailyTarget)
147:       const current = internal.state.data.completions[date]?.[activityId] ?? 0
148: 
149:       let next: number
150:       if (target > 0) {
151:         next = current < target ? current + 1 : current - 1
152:       } else {
153:         next = current > 0 ? 0 : 1
154:       }
155: 
156:       const dayCompletions = internal.state.data.completions[date] ?? {}
157:       if (next <= 0) {
158:         delete dayCompletions[activityId]
159:         if (Object.keys(dayCompletions).length === 0) {
160:           delete internal.state.data.completions[date]
161:         }
162:       } else {
163:         dayCompletions[activityId] = next
164:         internal.state.data.completions[date] = dayCompletions
165:       }
166:       persist(internal)
167:       emit(internal)
168:     },
169: 
170:     reload() {
171:       internal.state = { data: readStored(internal.backend), selectedDate: computeNow(internal) }
172:       persist(internal)
173:       emit(internal)
174:     },
175: 
176:     reset() {
177:       clearStored(internal.backend)
178:       internal.state = { data: readStored(internal.backend), selectedDate: computeNow(internal) }
179:       emit(internal)
180:     },
181:   }
182: }
183: 
184: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
185: // Hilfsfunktionen für die Ansicht
186: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
187: 
188: /** Anzahl erledigter Einträge einer Aktivität an einem Tag. */
189: export function getCompletedCount(
190:   data: StorageData,
191:   activityId: string,
192:   date: string,
193: ): number {
194:   return data.completions[date]?.[activityId] ?? 0
195: }
196: 
197: /** Ist eine Aktivität an einem Tag als (voll) erledigt? */
198: export function isCompleted(data: StorageData, activity: Activity, date: string): boolean {
199:   const count = getCompletedCount(data, activity.id, date)
200:   if (activity.dailyTarget > 0) {
201:     return count >= activity.dailyTarget
202:   }
203:   return count > 0
204: }
205: 
206: export interface CategoryStat {
207:   name: string
208:   total: number
209:   completed: number
210: }
211: 
212: export interface DayStats {
213:   total: number
214:   completed: number
215:   open: number
216:   percent: number
217:   byCategory: CategoryStat[]
218: }
219: 
220: /** Berechnet die Dashboard-Statistik für einen Tag. */
221: export function computeStats(data: StorageData, date: string): DayStats {
222:   const activities = Object.values(data.activities)
223:   const total = activities.length
224:   let completed = 0
225:   const byCategoryMap = new Map<string, { total: number; completed: number }>()
226: 
227:   for (const activity of activities) {
228:     const done = isCompleted(data, activity, date)
229:     if (done) {
230:       completed += 1
231:     }
232:     const cat = activity.category || 'Ohne Kategorie'
233:     const entry = byCategoryMap.get(cat) ?? { total: 0, completed: 0 }
234:     entry.total += 1
235:     if (done) {
236:       entry.completed += 1
237:     }
238:     byCategoryMap.set(cat, entry)
239:   }
240: 
241:   const byCategory = [...byCategoryMap.entries()]
242:     .map(([name, values]) => ({ name, ...values }))
243:     .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
244: 
245:   const open = total - completed
246:   const percent = total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((completed / total) * 100)
247: 
248:   return { total, completed, open, percent, byCategory }
249: }
250: 
251: /** Liefert sortierte, eindeutige Kategorien aus den aktuellen Aktivitäten. */
252: export function listCategories(data: StorageData): string[] {
253:   const categories = new Set<string>()
254:   for (const activity of Object.values(data.activities)) {
255:     if (activity.category) {
256:       categories.add(activity.category)
257:     }
258:   }
259:   return [...categories].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b))
260: }
261:

1: /**
2:  * Persistenzschicht für PulseBoard.
3:  *
4:  * Verantwortlichkeiten:
5:  * - Lesen/Schreiben einer versionierten Datenstruktur in einem Storage-Backend
6:  *   (Standard: localStorage).
7:  * - Migration von älteren Schema-Versionen (aktuell v0 -> v1).
8:  * - Robustheit: ungültige, veraltete oder beschädigte Daten dürfen nie zum
9:  *   Absturz führen. Stattdessen wird sicher ein leerer Ausgangszustand
10:  *   verwendet.
11:  */
12: 
13: import type { Activity, Completions, NewActivityInput, StorageData } from '../types'
14: import { STORAGE_KEY, STORAGE_VERSION } from '../types'
15: 
16: /** Minimaler Storage-Contract, um Tests mit einem In-Memory-Backend zu ermöglichen. */
17: export interface StorageBackend {
18:   get(key: string): string | null
19:   set(key: string, value: string): void
20:   remove(key: string): void
21: }
22: 
23: export const defaultBackend: StorageBackend = {
24:   get: (key) => localStorage.getItem(key),
25:   set: (key, value) => localStorage.setItem(key, value),
26:   remove: (key) => localStorage.removeItem(key),
27: }
28: 
29: /** Erstellt einen sauberen, leeren Ausgangszustand der aktuellen Version. */
30: export function createEmptyData(): StorageData {
31:   return { version: STORAGE_VERSION, activities: {}, completions: {} }
32: }
33: 
34: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
35: // Validierung & Migration
36: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
37: 
38: function isValidActivity(value: unknown): value is Activity {
39:   if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) {
40:     return false
41:   }
42:   const a = value as Record<string, unknown>
43:   return (
44:     typeof a.id === 'string' &&
45:     typeof a.name === 'string' &&
46:     typeof a.description === 'string' &&
47:     typeof a.category === 'string' &&
48:     typeof a.dailyTarget === 'number' &&
49:     Number.isFinite(a.dailyTarget) &&
50:     typeof a.createdAt === 'string'
51:   )
52: }
53: 
54: function normalizeActivities(value: unknown): Record<string, Activity> {
55:   const result: Record<string, Activity> = {}
56:   if (value == null) {
57:     return result
58:   }
59:   const entries = Array.isArray(value) ? value : Object.values(value as object)
60:   for (const entry of entries) {
61:     if (isValidActivity(entry)) {
62:       // Sicherheit: Tagesziel nie negativ.
63:       entry.dailyTarget = Math.max(0, entry.dailyTarget)
64:       result[entry.id] = entry
65:     }
66:   }
67:   return result
68: }
69: 
70: function normalizeCompletions(value: unknown): Completions {
71:   const result: Completions = {}
72:   if (value == null || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) {
73:     return result
74:   }
75:   for (const [date, perDay] of Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)) {
76:     if (typeof perDay !== 'object' || perDay == null || Array.isArray(perDay)) {
77:       continue
78:     }
79:     const counts: Record<string, number> = {}
80:     for (const [activityId, count] of Object.entries(perDay as Record<string, unknown>)) {
81:       if (typeof count === 'number' && Number.isFinite(count) && count > 0) {
82:         counts[activityId] = Math.floor(count)
83:       }
84:     }
85:     if (Object.keys(counts).length > 0) {
86:       result[date] = counts
87:     }
88:   }
89:   return result
90: }
91: 
92: /**
93:  * Konvertiert eine v0-Datenstruktur (Array-basiert) in das v1-Objekt-Format.
94:  * Gibt `null` zurück, wenn die Grundstruktur nicht verwendbar ist.
95:  */
96: function migrateV0(parsed: Record<string, unknown>): StorageData | null {
97:   const activities = normalizeActivities(parsed.activities)
98:   const completions = normalizeCompletions(parsed.completions)
99:   return { version: STORAGE_VERSION, activities, completions }
100: }
101: 
102: /**
103:  * Validiert und migriert einen geparsten Wert.
104:  * Gibt `null` zurück, wenn die Struktur grundsätzlich unhambarrlich ist
105:  * (z. B. Zahl, String, Array auf Top-Ebene, unbekannte Version).
106:  */
107: export function parseStored(raw: string | null): StorageData | null {
108:   if (!raw) {
109:     return createEmptyData()
110:   }
111: 
112:   let parsed: unknown
113:   try {
114:     parsed = JSON.parse(raw)
115:   } catch {
116:     // Beschädigtes JSON -> sicher leer, kein Absturz.
117:     return null
118:   }
119: 
120:   if (typeof parsed !== 'object' || parsed === null || Array.isArray(parsed)) {
121:     return null
122:   }
123: 
124:   const record = parsed as Record<string, unknown>
125:   const version = typeof record.version === 'number' ? record.version : STORAGE_VERSION
126: 
127:   if (version === STORAGE_VERSION) {
128:     const activities = normalizeActivities(record.activities)
129:     const completions = normalizeCompletions(record.completions)
130:     return { version: STORAGE_VERSION, activities, completions }
131:   }
132: 
133:   if (version === 0) {
134:     return migrateV0(record)
135:   }
136: 
137:   // Unbekannte/zukünftige Version -> zurücksetzen, aber nicht abstürzen.
138:   return null
139: }
140: 
141: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
142: // Lese-/Schreiboperationen
143: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
144: 
145: /** Liest und normalisiert die gespeicherten Daten aus dem Backend. */
146: export function readStored(backend: StorageBackend = defaultBackend): StorageData {
147:   const parsed = parseStored(backend.getItem(STORAGE_KEY))
148:   return parsed ?? createEmptyData()
149: }
150: 
151: /** Schreibt die Daten serialisiert ins Backend und fängt Fehler ab. */
152: export function writeStored(data: StorageData, backend: StorageBackend = defaultBackend): void {
153:   try {
154:     backend.set(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data))
155:   } catch {
156:     // Z. B. Speicherlimit oder gesperrter Storage: nicht kritisch für die App.
157:   }
158: }
159: 
160: export function clearStored(backend: StorageBackend = defaultBackend): void {
161:   try {
162:     backend.remove(STORAGE_KEY)
163:   } catch {
164:     // ignore
165:   }
166: }
167: 
168: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
169: // Validierung von Aktivitäts-Eingaben
170: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
171: 
172: export interface ValidatedActivity {
173:   ok: true
174:   activity: Activity
175: }
176: 
177: export interface InvalidActivity {
178:   ok: false
179:   error: string
180: }
181: 
182: export type ValidationResult = ValidatedActivity | InvalidActivity
183: 
184: /**
185:  * Prüft und normalisiert eine Aktivitäts-Eingabe.
186:  * Gibt eine Fehlermeldung zurück, wenn die Eingabe ungült ist.
187:  */
188: export function validateActivity(
189:   input: NewActivityInput,
190:   overrides: Partial<Activity>,
191: ): ValidationResult {
192:   const name = (input.name ?? '').trim()
193:   const category = (input.category ?? '').trim()
194:   const description = (input.description ?? '').trim()
195: 
196:   let dailyTarget = 0
197:   if (input.dailyTarget !== undefined && input.dailyTarget !== null) {
198:     const value = Number(input.dailyTarget)
199:     if (Number.isFinite(value) && value >= 0) {
200:       dailyTarget = Math.floor(value)
201:     } else {
202:       return { ok: false, error: 'Tagesziel muss eine Zahl größer oder gleich 0 sein.' }
203:     }
204:   }
205: 
206:   if (name.length === 0) {
207:     return { ok: false, error: 'Bitte gib einen Name ein.' }
208:   }
209:   if (category.length === 0) {
210:     return { ok: false, error: 'Bitte wähle eine Kategorie aus.' }
211:   }
212: 
213:   return {
214:     ok: true,
215:     activity: {
216:       id: overrides.id ?? crypto.randomUUID(),
217:       name,
218:       description,
219:       category,
220:       dailyTarget,
221:       createdAt: overrides.createdAt ?? new Date().toISOString(),
222:     },
223:   }
224: }

1: /**
2:  * Hilfsfunktionen für Datumsberechnungen.
3:  * Intern werden Datumsketten im Format YYYY-MM-DD (lokal) verwendet, damit
4:  * verschiedene Kalendertage sauber getrennt bleiben und es durch
5:  * Zeitzonen-/Sommerzeit-Umbuchungen keine Randfälle gibt.
6:  */
7: 
8: /** Liefert den aktuellen Tag als YYYY-MM-DD-Kette (lokal). */
9: export function todayKey(date: Date = new Date()): string {
10:   return dateToKey(date)
11: }
12: 
13: /** Wandelt ein lokales Date-Objekt in eine YYYY-MM-DD-Kette um. */
14: export function dateToKey(date: Date): string {
15:   const y = date.getFullYear()
16:   const m = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')
17:   const d = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')
18:   return `${y}-${m}-${d}`
19: }
20: 
21: /** Wandelt eine YYYY-MM-DD-Kette in ein lokales Date (Mitternacht) um. */
22: export function keyToDate(key: string): Date {
23:   const [y, m, d] = key.split('-').map(Number)
24:   // Monatsindex in JS ist 0-basiert.
25:   return new Date(y, m - 1, d)
26: }
27: 
28: /** Addiert (oder subtrahiert) ganze Tage zu einer Kette. vermeidet DST-Sprünge. */
29: export function addDays(key: string, days: number): string {
30:   const date = keyToDate(key)
31:   date.setDate(date.getDate() + days)
32:   return dateToKey(date)
33: }
34: 
35: /** Ist der Tag strictly nach dem heutigen Tag? */
36: export function isFuture(key: string, today: string = todayKey()): boolean {
37:   return key > today
38: }
39: 
40: /** Ist der Tag der heutige? */
41: export function isToday(key: string, today: string = todayKey()): boolean {
42:   return key === today
43: }
44: 
45: /**
46:  * Menschlich lesbares Label für einen Tag relativ zu "heute".
47:  * Nutzt Intl.DateTimeFormat für eine saubere, lokale Formatierung.
48:  */
49: export function formatDayLabel(key: string, today: string = todayKey()): string {
50:   const date = keyToDate(key)
51:   const opts: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions = {
52:     weekday: 'long',
53:     day: 'numeric',
54:     month: 'long',
55:   }
56: 
57:   if (isToday(key, today)) {
58:     return 'Heute'
59:   }
60: 
61:   const yesterday = addDays(today, -1)
62:   const tomorrow = addDays(today, 1)
63:   if (key === yesterday) return 'Gestern'
64:   if (key === tomorrow) return 'Morgen'
65: 
66:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('de-DE', opts).format(date)
67: }
68: 
69: /** Saubere, kurze Formatierung z. B. für die Navigationsleiste. */
70: export function formatShort(key: string): string {
71:   const date = keyToDate(key)
72:   return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('de-DE', {
73:     day: '2-digit',
74:     month: '2-digit',
75:     year: 'numeric',
76:   }).format(date)
77: }

9:   editActivity: (id: string, input: ActivityInput) => boolean

16: /**
17:  * React-Wrapper um die reine Speicherfabrik. Haltet den Zustand synchron und
18:  * schreibt Änderungen persist in den Storage.
19:  */
20: export function useAppStore(): AppState & AppActions {
21:   const store = useMemo<Store>(() => createStore(), [])
22: 
23:   const [state, setState] = useState<AppState>(() => store.getState())
24: 
25:   useEffect(() => {
26:     return store.subscribe(() => setState(store.getState()))
27:   }, [store])
28: 
29:   const actions = useMemo<AppActions>(
30:     () => ({
31:       setSelectedDate: (date) => store.setSelectedDate(date),
32:       addActivity: (input) => store.addActivity(input),
33:       editActivity: (id, input) => store.editActivity(id, input),
34:       deleteActivity: (id) => store.deleteActivity(id),
35:       toggleComplete: (activityId, date) => store.toggleComplete(activityId, date),
36:       reload: () => store.reload(),
37:       reset: () => store.reset(),
38:     }),
39:     [store],
40:   )
41: 
42:   return { ...state, ...actions }
43: }

1: import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
2: 
3: export type ThemeChoice = 'system' | 'light' | 'dark'
4: 
5: const THEME_KEY = 'pulseboard-theme'
6: 
7: function getInitialChoice(): ThemeChoice {
8:   try {
9:     const stored = localStorage.getItem(THEME_KEY)
10:     if (stored === 'light' || stored === 'dark' || stored === 'system') {
11:       return stored
12:     }
13:   } catch {
14:     // ignore
15:   }
16:   return 'system'
17: }
18: 
19: function resolveTheme(choice: ThemeChoice): 'light' | 'dark' {
20:   if (choice === 'light') return 'light'
21:   if (choice === 'dark') return 'dark'
22:   const prefersDark =
23:     typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
24:     window.matchMedia &&
25:     window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches
26:   return prefersDark ? 'dark' : 'light'
27: }
28: 
29: function applyTheme(theme: 'light' | 'dark'): void {
30:   const root = document.documentElement
31:   root.setAttribute('data-theme', theme)
32:   const meta = document.querySelector<HTMLMetaElement>('meta[name="theme-color"]')
33:   if (meta) {
34:     meta.content = theme === 'dark' ? '#0f172a' : '#f8fafc'
35:   }
36: }
37: 
38: /**
39:  * Verwählt und synchronisiert den Dark/Light-Modus. Standard ist das System-
40:  * Präferenz, die Wahl wird lokal gespeichert.
41:  */
42: export function useTheme(): { choice: ThemeChoice; setChoice: (c: ThemeChoice) => void } {
43:   const [choice, setChoice] = useState<ThemeChoice>(getInitialChoice)
44: 
45:   useEffect(() => {
46:     const theme = resolveTheme(choice)
47:     applyTheme(theme)
48:     try {
49:       localStorage.setItem(THEME_KEY, choice)
50:     } catch {
51:       // ignore
52:     }
53:   }, [choice])
54: 
55:   return { choice, setChoice }
56: }

4: /** Umschalter zwischen Light-/Dark-Modus (klickbar, keyboard-freundlich). */
5: export function ThemeToggle() {
6:   const { choice, setChoice } = useTheme()
7:   const isDark = choice === 'dark' || (choice === 'system' && darkModeMatches())
8: 
9:   return (
10:     <button
11:       type="button"
12:       className="icon-btn"
13:       onClick={() => setChoice(isDark ? 'light' : 'dark')}
14:       aria-label={isDark ? 'Hellen Modus wählen' : 'Dunkelen Modus wählen'}
15:       aria-pressed={isDark}
16:       title={choice === 'system' ? 'System' : choice}
17:     >
18:       {isDark ? <MoonIcon /> : <SunIcon />}
19:     </button>
20:   )
21: }
22: 
23: function darkModeMatches(): boolean {
24:   if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !window.matchMedia) return false
25:   return window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches
26: }

=== README ===
# React + TypeScript + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some Oxlint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the Oxlint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend enabling type-aware lint rules by installing `oxlint-tsgolint` and editing `.oxlintrc.json`:

```json
{
  "$schema": "./node_modules/oxlint/configuration_schema.json",
  "plugins": ["react", "typescript", "oxc"],
  "options": {
    "typeAware": true
  },
  "rules": {
    "react/rules-of-hooks": "error",
    "react/only-export-components": ["warn", { "allowConstantExport": true }]
  }
}
```
