38: function isValidActivity(value: unknown): value is Activity {
39:   if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null) {
40:     return false
41:   }
42:   const a = value as Record<string, unknown>
43:   return (
44:     typeof a.id === 'string' &&
45:     typeof a.name === 'string' &&
46:     typeof a.description === 'string' &&
47:     typeof a.category === 'string' &&
48:     typeof a.dailyTarget === 'number' &&
49:     Number.isFinite(a.dailyTarget) &&
50:     typeof a.createdAt === 'string'
51:   )
52: }
53: 
54: function normalizeActivities(value: unknown): Record<string, Activity> {
55:   const result: Record<string, Activity> = {}
56:   if (value == null) {
57:     return result
58:   }
59:   const entries = Array.isArray(value) ? value : Object.values(value as object)
60:   for (const entry of entries) {
61:     if (isValidActivity(entry)) {
62:       // Sicherheit: Tagesziel nie negativ.
63:       entry.dailyTarget = Math.max(0, entry.dailyTarget)
64:       result[entry.id] = entry
65:     }
66:   }
67:   return result
68: }
69: 
70: function normalizeCompletions(value: unknown): Completions {
71:   const result: Completions = {}
72:   if (value == null || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) {
73:     return result
74:   }
75:   for (const [date, perDay] of Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)) {
76:     if (typeof perDay !== 'object' || perDay == null || Array.isArray(perDay)) {
77:       continue
78:     }
79:     const counts: Record<string, number> = {}
80:     for (const [activityId, count] of Object.entries(perDay as Record<string, unknown>)) {
81:       if (typeof count === 'number' && Number.isFinite(count) && count > 0) {
82:         counts[activityId] = Math.floor(count)
83:       }
84:     }
85:     if (Object.keys(counts).length > 0) {
86:       result[date] = counts
87:     }
88:   }
89:   return result
90: }

94:  * Gibt `null` zurück, wenn die Grundstruktur nicht verwendbar ist.
95:  */
96: function migrateV0(parsed: Record<string, unknown>): StorageData | null {
97:   const activities = normalizeActivities(parsed.activities)
98:   const completions = normalizeCompletions(parsed.completions)
99:   return { version: STORAGE_VERSION, activities, completions }
100: }
101: 
102: /**
103:  * Validiert und migriert einen geparsten Wert.

105:  * (z. B. Zahl, String, Array auf Top-Ebene, unbekannte Version).
106:  */
107: export function parseStored(raw: string | null): StorageData | null {
108:   if (!raw) {
109:     return createEmptyData()
110:   }
111: 
112:   let parsed: unknown
113:   try {
114:     parsed = JSON.parse(raw)
115:   } catch {
116:     // Beschädigtes JSON -> sicher leer, kein Absturz.
117:     return null
118:   }
119: 
120:   if (typeof parsed !== 'object' || parsed === null || Array.isArray(parsed)) {
121:     return null
122:   }
123: 
124:   const record = parsed as Record<string, unknown>
125:   const version = typeof record.version === 'number' ? record.version : STORAGE_VERSION
126: 
127:   if (version === STORAGE_VERSION) {
128:     const activities = normalizeActivities(record.activities)
129:     const completions = normalizeCompletions(record.completions)
130:     return { version: STORAGE_VERSION, activities, completions }
131:   }
132: 
133:   if (version === 0) {
134:     return migrateV0(record)
135:   }
136: 
137:   // Unbekannte/zukünftige Version -> zurücksetzen, aber nicht abstürzen.
138:   return null
139: }

6:  * Geschäftslogik (Erstellen/Bearbeiten/Löschen, Tagesstatus, Datumswechsel,

85: 
86:     subscribe(listener) {

97:     addActivity(input) {

99:       if (!result.ok) {
100:         return null
101:       }
102:       const activity = result.activity
103:       internal.state.data.activities[activity.id] = activity
104:       persist(internal)
105:       emit(internal)

107:     },
108: 
109:     editActivity(id, input) {
110:       const existing = internal.state.data.activities[id]
111:       if (!existing) {
112:         return false
113:       }
114:       const result = validateActivity(input, { id, createdAt: existing.createdAt })
115:       if (!result.ok) {
116:         return false
117:       }
118:       internal.state.data.activities[id] = result.activity
119:       persist(internal)
120:       emit(internal)
121:       return true
122:     },
123: 
124:     deleteActivity(id) {
125:       if (!internal.state.data.activities[id]) {
126:         return
127:       }
128:       delete internal.state.data.activities[id]
129:       // Verwaigte Zählstände entfernen.

131:         delete dateKeys[id]

136: 
137:     toggleComplete(activityId, date) {
138:       const activity = internal.state.data.activities[activityId]
139:       if (!activity) {
140:         return
141:       }
142:       // Zukünftige Tage dürfen nicht als erledigt markiert werden.
143:       if (isFuture(date, computeNow(internal))) {
144:         return
145:       }
146:       const target = Math.max(0, activity.dailyTarget)
147:       const current = internal.state.data.completions[date]?.[activityId] ?? 0
148: 
149:       let next: number
150:       if (target > 0) {
151:         next = current < target ? current + 1 : current - 1
152:       } else {
153:         next = current > 0 ? 0 : 1
154:       }
155: 
156:       const dayCompletions = internal.state.data.completions[date] ?? {}
157:       if (next <= 0) {
158:         delete dayCompletions[activityId]
159:         if (Object.keys(dayCompletions).length === 0) {
160:           delete internal.state.data.completions[date]
161:         }
162:       } else {
163:         dayCompletions[activityId] = next
164:         internal.state.data.completions[date] = dayCompletions
165:       }
166:       persist(internal)
167:       emit(internal)
168:     },
169: 
170:     reload() {
171:       internal.state = { data: readStored(internal.backend), selectedDate: computeNow(internal) }
172:       persist(internal)
173:       emit(internal)
174:     },
175: 
176:     reset() {
177:       clearStored(internal.backend)
178:       internal.state = { data: readStored(internal.backend), selectedDate: computeNow(internal) }
179:       emit(internal)
180:     },
181:   }
182: }

185: // Hilfsfunktionen für die Ansicht
186: // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
187: 
188: /** Anzahl erledigter Einträge einer Aktivität an einem Tag. */
189: export function getCompletedCount(
190:   data: StorageData,
191:   activityId: string,
192:   date: string,
193: ): number {
194:   return data.completions[date]?.[activityId] ?? 0
195: }
196: 
197: /** Ist eine Aktivität an einem Tag als (voll) erledigt? */
198: export function isCompleted(data: StorageData, activity: Activity, date: string): boolean {
199:   const count = getCompletedCount(data, activity.id, date)
200:   if (activity.dailyTarget > 0) {
201:     return count >= activity.dailyTarget
202:   }
203:   return count > 0
204: }
205: 
206: export interface CategoryStat {
207:   name: string
208:   total: number
209:   completed: number
210: }
211: 
212: export interface DayStats {
213:   total: number
214:   completed: number
215:   open: number
216:   percent: number
217:   byCategory: CategoryStat[]
218: }
219: 
220: /** Berechnet die Dashboard-Statistik für einen Tag. */
221: export function computeStats(data: StorageData, date: string): DayStats {
222:   const activities = Object.values(data.activities)
223:   const total = activities.length
224:   let completed = 0
225:   const byCategoryMap = new Map<string, { total: number; completed: number }>()
226: 
227:   for (const activity of activities) {
228:     const done = isCompleted(data, activity, date)
229:     if (done) {
230:       completed += 1
231:     }
232:     const cat = activity.category || 'Ohne Kategorie'
233:     const entry = byCategoryMap.get(cat) ?? { total: 0, completed: 0 }
234:     entry.total += 1
235:     if (done) {
236:       entry.completed += 1
237:     }
238:     byCategoryMap.set(cat, entry)
239:   }
240: 
241:   const byCategory = [...byCategoryMap.entries()]
242:     .map(([name, values]) => ({ name, ...values }))
243:     .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
244: 
245:   const open = total - completed
246:   const percent = total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((completed / total) * 100)
247: 
248:   return { total, completed, open, percent, byCategory }
249: }
250: 
251: /** Liefert sortierte, eindeutige Kategorien aus den aktuellen Aktivitäten. */
252: export function listCategories(data: StorageData): string[] {
253:   const categories = new Set<string>()
254:   for (const activity of Object.values(data.activities)) {
255:     if (activity.category) {
256:       categories.add(activity.category)
257:     }
258:   }
259:   return [...categories].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b))
260: }
